C is an integer with C ≤ 2 (LFR I p.191 l.23–24). Every Ω_m is a union of MR_m-cubes of the scale m, from mutually compatible partitions (CRE p.264 l.53–55), where R_m is a power of L with R_m ≥ (log g_m^{−2})^r > 1 for g small (CRE (2.5), p.255 l.36–38), so R_m ≥ L. A domain made by a renormalization transformation is such a union (CRE p.256 l.21–23). An R-operation at the step i keeps this. Its admissible sequences of M-cubes (LFR I (1.10), p.179 l.19–24) lie inside its region Z and belong to the intermediate layering 𝐁″_i. The post-R layering, on which 𝒟^λ_i is posed, puts all of Z on the layer i (LFR II (1.33), p.365 l.2–7), and on Z^c it is the layering before the operation (LFR I (1.12), p.179 l.35–36). So the post-R domains are Ω_m ∪ Z for m ≤ i. Z is a union of components of Λ_i^c (LFR I p.177 l.17, l.33), and Λ_i is a union of MR_i-cubes of the scale i, so Z is a union of MR_i-cubes, and so of MR_m-cubes of the scale m for every m ≤ i: the side ratio L^{i−m}R_i/R_m is a power of L and at least 1, since for i > m, R_m < L(log g_m^{−2})^r ≤ L(1+β₀)(i−m)^{β₀}(log g_i^{−2})^r ≤ L(1+β₀)(i−m)^{β₀}R_i < L^{i−m+1}R_i for L ≥ 2 and β₀ ≤ ½ (CRE (2.5), p.255 l.36–38, R_m being the least power of L above the bound; (2.7), p.255 l.56–57, as at #6.17.1), and the partitions are compatible. The distance from Ω_{n+1}^c to Ω_{n+2} is at least ML^{n+1}ξ = La ≥ 8a. A renormalization transformation at the step m + 1 builds Ω_{m+1} outside (B^{m+1}(P^1_{m+1})^c)^{∼3} (CRE (3.5), p.265 l.43), with B^{m+1}(P^1_{m+1}) ⊂ (Z_m′^{∼5})^c (p.265 l.18–19) and Z_m′ ⊇ Z_m = Λ_m^c (p.264 l.52–53) ⊇ Ω_m^c (CRE (2.1), p.254 l.50), so Ω_{m+1} is at least eight layers of MR_{m+1}-cubes of the scale m + 1 from Ω_m^c, whether or not an R-operation has made Λ_m = Ω_m (CRE p.255 l.1–3). An R-operation at the step i ≥ n + 2 adds Z to Ω_{n+1} and to Ω_{n+2}. A lattice neighbour of Z outside Z lies in Λ_i ⊂ Ω_i ⊂ Ω_{n+2}. For p ∈ Z and q ∉ Ω_{n+1} ∪ Z, a monotone nearest-neighbour path from p to q leaves Z at a point r ∈ Ω_{n+2} whose coordinates lie between those of p and q, so abs(p − q) ≥ abs(r − q) ≥ dist(Ω_{n+1}^c, Ω_{n+2}) before the operation.
